4. РџРѕРїСЂРѕР±СѓР№С‚Рµ РѕС‚РєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ Р°РЅС‚РёРІРёСЂСѓСЃ (РµСЃР»Рё РїРѕРјРѕРіР»Рѕ, С‚Рѕ РґРѕР±Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ exe РІ РёСЃРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЏ). РќРµ Р·Р°Р±СѓРґСЊС‚Рµ РІРєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ Р°РЅС‚РёРІРёСЂСѓСЃ РѕР±СЂР°С‚РЅРѕ.

**A**: РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚Рµ РІ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР°С… РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё РІ РїРѕР»Рµ **DC в†’ IP** РІСЃС‘, РєСЂРѕРјРµ `4:149.154.167.220`. Р•СЃР»Рё СЌС‚Рѕ РЅРµ РїРѕРјРѕРіР»Рѕ, РїРѕР»РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊСЋ РѕС‡РёСЃС‚РёС‚Рµ СЌС‚Рѕ РїРѕР»Рµ.

## РЈ РІР°СЃ РЅРµ РІРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅ CF-РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё, РІРєР»СЋС‡РёС‚Рµ РµРіРѕ РІ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР°С….

Р•СЃР»Рё РІ Р»РѕРіР°С… Р±СѓРґРµС‚ **TimeoutError**, С‚Рѕ:

Timeout error РїСЂРё CF-РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё - РїСЂРѕР±Р»РµРјР° СЃ РІР°С€РµР№ СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅС‹. Р РљРќ/СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅРЅРёРµ РІРїРЅС‹/РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё Рё С‚.Рї.

РЈРєР°Р·Р°РЅ **РЅРµРІРµСЂРЅС‹Р№ СЃРµРєСЂРµС‚** РІ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР°С… РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё РІ С‚РµР»РµРіСЂР°РјРµ

`Р›РєРј` РїРѕ РёРєРѕРЅРєРµ РІ С‚СЂРµРµ -> **РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РІ Telegram**
=== FILE: .github\scripts\check_log.py ===
import os
from time import sleep
import urllib.request as request

url = os.environ["LOG_URL"]
path_answer = ".github/scripts/answers"

for _ in range(0,3):
    try:
        with request.urlopen(url,timeout=30.0) as r:
            log_bytes = r.read()
        break 
    except Exception:
        sleep(5.0)
else:
    print(str(path_answer + "/default.md"))
    exit()

log_old = log_bytes.decode("utf-8",errors="replace").splitlines()

worker_end = False
cfproxy_end = False

log = []
for line in reversed(log_old):
    line = line[20:]
    log.append(line)
    if "CF proxy:      enabled" in line:
        cfproxy_end = True
    elif "CF worker:     enabled" in line:
        worker_end = True
    elif "INFO   tg-ws-tray  Starting proxy" in line:
        break

secret_false = 0
timeouterror = 0
reset_error = 0 

for line in log :
    if "(wrong secret or proto)" in line:
        secret_false += 1 
        if secret_false >= 10:
            print(str(path_answer + "/wrong_secret.md"))
            break
    elif "TimeoutError" in line: 
        timeouterror += 1 
        if timeouterror >= 20:
            if cfproxy_end is False:
                print(str(path_answer + "/no_cfproxy.md"))
                break
            if worker_end is False:
                print(str(path_answer + "/timeout_no_worker.md"))
            else:
                print(str(path_answer + "/timeout.md"))                  
            break
    elif "ConnectionResetError" in line:
        reset_error += 1 
        if reset_error >= 20:
            print(str(path_answer + "/reset_error.md"))
            break
else: 
    if cfproxy_end is True:
        print(str(path_answer + "/default.md"))
    else:
        print(str(path_answer + "/no_cfproxy.md"))

=== FILE: docs\EN\BuildFromSource.md ===
# Building from Source

## Console Proxy

To run only the proxy without the system tray interface, basic installation is sufficient:

```bash
pip install -e .
tg-ws-proxy
```

## Tray Application by OS

### Windows 7/10+

```bash
pip install -e .
tg-ws-proxy-tray-win
```

### macOS

Requires a Python build with Tk support. You can verify it with the command `python3 -m tkinter`.

```bash
pip install -e .
tg-ws-proxy-tray-macos
```

### Linux

```bash
pip install -e .
tg-ws-proxy-tray-linux
```

## Console Mode from Source

```bash
tg-ws-proxy [--port PORT] [--host HOST] [--dc-ip DC:IP ...] [-v]
```

**Arguments:**

| Argument | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `--port` | `1443` | Proxy port |
| `--host` | `127.0.0.1` | Proxy host |
| `--secret` | `random` | 32-character hex key for client authorization |
| `--dc-ip` | `2:149.154.167.220`, `4:149.154.167.220` | Target IP for DC (can be specified multiple times) |
| `--no-cfproxy` | `false` | Disable [Cloudflare proxying](./CfProxy.md) attempts |
| `--no-h2` | `false` | Disable HTTP/2 media multiplexing through CF proxy |
| `--cfproxy-domain` | | Specify your own domain for Cloudflare proxying [Learn more](./CfProxy.md). Can be specified multiple times. |
| `--cfproxy-worker-domain` | | Cloudflare Worker domain [Learn more](./CfWorker.md). Can be specified multiple times. |
| `--no-secure` | `false` | Use 80 port for CF-proxy and CF-worker connections |
| `--fake-tls-domain` | | Enable Fake TLS masquerading (ee-secret) with specified SNI domain |
| `--proxy-protocol` | disabled | Accept HAProxy PROXY protocol v1 (for use behind nginx/haproxy with `proxy_protocol on`) |
| `--force-test-dc` | `false` | Route all traffic to Telegram test DCs [Learn more](./TestDc.md) |
| `--buf-kb` | `256` | Buffer size in KB |
| `--pool-size` | `4` | Number of ready WS connections per DC. `0` disables the direct DCв†’IP WS route |
| `--log-file` | disabled | Path to file for saving logs |
| `--log-max-mb` | `5` | Maximum log file size in MB (afterwards overwrites) |
| `--log-backups` | `1` | Number of rotated log files to keep (minimum 1) |
| `-v`, `--verbose` | disabled | Verbose logging (DEBUG) |

**Examples:**

```bash
# Standard startup
tg-ws-proxy --secret 00112233445566778899aabbccddeeff

# Different port and additional DCs
tg-ws-proxy --port 9050 --dc-ip 1:149.154.175.205 --dc-ip 2:149.154.167.220

# With verbose logging, without direct connections to DCs
tg-ws-proxy -v --dc-ip

# Fake TLS masquerading (ee-secret)
tg-ws-proxy --fake-tls-domain example.com
```

=== FILE: docs\EN\CfProxy.md ===
# Cloudflare Proxy

An alternative, free connection method is proxying through Cloudflare, which can be used for unreachable data centers. **All you need to get it working is a domain**. The application includes a default domain, but it can (and ideally should) be replaced with your own.
  
The proxy restores access to content that previously wouldn't load (reactions, certain stickers). If you are using a non-Premium account and photos/videos still fail to load, leave only `4:149.154.167.220` in the `DC в†’ IP` block. If the CF proxy works, media will start loading again.

## Why should I set up my own domain?

Cloudflare limits the number of simultaneous WebSocket (WS) connections. The default domain could stop working at any moment.

## Setting up your own domain

1. Add your domain to Cloudflare (either by purchasing it directly from Cloudflare or by changing the NS servers: https://developers.cloudflare.com/dns/zone-setups/full-setup/setup/). Domains cost around $1.50вЂ“$2.00 per year, and any domain extension will work.

2. In `SSL/TLS` в†’ `Overview`, set the mode to **Flexible**.

3. In `DNS` в†’ `Records`, add the following `A` records via `+ Add Record`:
- Name=`kws1`   IPv4=`149.154.175.50`
- Name=`kws2`   IPv4=`149.154.167.51`
- Name=`kws3`   IPv4=`149.154.175.100`
- Name=`kws4`   IPv4=`149.154.167.91`
- Name=`kws5`   IPv4=`149.154.171.5`
- Name=`kws203` IPv4=`91.105.192.100`

4. **Add your domain to [zapret](https://github.com/Flowseal/zapret-discord-youtube/) or any other DPI bypass software, as the Cloudflare subnet may be blocked (e.g., in Russia).**

5. In the `TgWsProxy` settings, replace the default domain with your own.

## HTTP/2 media multiplexing

Multiplexing is enabled by default. Media connections share one TCP/TLS connection to Cloudflare per DC and domain, using `/api` instead of `/apiws`. Main connections and test DCs continue using WS. H2 requires CF proxy and TLS enabled.  
Can be disabled with `--no-h2` or by unchecking the **Media multiplexing (HTTP/2)** in the **Cloudflare Proxy** settings. 

## Credits / Acknowledgments

- Original Idea: https://github.com/Nekogram/WSProxy
- Special thanks to [@UjuiUjuMandan](https://github.com/UjuiUjuMandan) for providing the information.

=== FILE: docs\EN\CfWorker.md ===
# Cloudflare Worker

An alternative (completely free, no domain purchase required unlike [CfProxy](./CfProxy.md)) method for proxying.

The proxy restores access to content that previously wouldn't load (reactions, certain stickers). If you are using a non-Premium account with this method and photos/videos still fail to load, leave only `4:149.154.167.220` in the `DC в†’ IP` block.

##

1. **Add the following domains to [zapret](https://github.com/Flowseal/zapret-discord-youtube/) or any other DPI bypass software:**

```
cloudflare.com
cloudflare.dev
workers.dev
```

2. Create an account on [Cloudflare](https://dash.cloudflare.com/) (or log into an existing one)
	* **After creating your account, verify your email using the link sent to your inbox**
3. Select `Compute` в†’ `Workers & Pages` from the left panel
   <img width="250" height="768" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/d81e3522-045a-4e65-9c2e-5545b7ad409a" />

4. Click the **`Create application`** button in the top right в†’ `Start with Hello World!` в†’ `Deploy`
   <img width="1406" height="193" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/7ac65944-8761-42a6-ab6d-ba5f9080c883" />
   <img width="586" height="379" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/ff901439-c2a1-4867-95de-e11b82a37044" />
   <img width="624" height="694" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/bb68d49a-166d-42a0-8fe2-bd2b16c0d066" />

5. Click the **`Edit code`** button in the top right, then replace the code on the left with the one [found at the bottom of this page](#worker-code)
    * If the code section fails to load, it means you missed the first step
    <img width="911" height="117" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/6bcdf839-d776-47e9-9d18-ba0efdf53244" />
	<img width="1027" height="512" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/daf131ed-82d5-40f0-a7eb-daeb598bea40" />


6. Click the **`Deploy`** button in the top right
   <img width="415" height="138" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/58d8f83e-d8b5-40cf-a30f-741d7311047b" />

7. Copy the domain from the field on the right and specify it in your **Cloudflare Worker** settings (or via the `--cfproxy-worker-domain` argument)
    * Example domain: `random-symbols-1234.username.workers.dev`
	* **You can specify multiple domains separated by commas (or by repeating the `--cfproxy-worker-domain` argument)**
   <img width="414" height="182" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/4fb0b111-8026-4d17-b993-6c70ec37f1f5" />



### Worker Code

```javascript
import { connect } from "cloudflare:sockets";

function toBytes(data) {
	if (data instanceof ArrayBuffer) {
		return new Uint8Array(data);
	}
	if (typeof data === "string") {
		return new TextEncoder().encode(data);
	}
	if (data && typeof data.arrayBuffer === "function") {
		return data.arrayBuffer().then((ab) => new Uint8Array(ab));
	}
	return new Uint8Array();
}

export default {
	async fetch(request) {
		if ((request.headers.get("Upgrade") || "").toLowerCase() !== "websocket") {
			return new Response("Expected websocket", { status: 426 });
		}

		const url = new URL(request.url);
		if (url.pathname !== "/apiws") {
			return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
		}

		const dst = url.searchParams.get("dst");
		const pair = new WebSocketPair();
		const client = pair[0];
		const server = pair[1];
		server.accept();

		const socket = connect({ hostname: dst, port: 443 });
		const tcpReader = socket.readable.getReader();
		const tcpWriter = socket.writable.getWriter();

		server.addEventListener("message", async (event) => {
			try {
				await tcpWriter.write(await toBytes(event.data));
			} catch {
				try {
					server.close(1011, "tcp write failed");
				} catch {}
			}
		});

		server.addEventListener("close", async () => {
			try {
				await tcpWriter.close();
			} catch {}
			try {
				socket.close();
			} catch {}
		});

		(async () => {
			try {
				while (true) {
					const { value, done } = await tcpReader.read();
					if (done) {
						break;
					}
					if (value) {
						server.send(value);
					}
				}
			} catch {
			} finally {
				try {
					server.close();
				} catch {}
				try {
					tcpReader.releaseLock();
				} catch {}
				try {
					socket.close();
				} catch {}
			}
		})();

		return new Response(null, { status: 101, webSocket: client });
	},
};
```

# CONTRIBUTING

Thank you for wanting to help the `tg-ws-proxy` project.

## Before Creating an Issue

1. Check the documentation in `docs/README.md`.
2. Make sure a similar issue hasn't already been opened.
3. Use standard labels from `.github/labels.md` for correct triage.

## How to Report Problems

- Use the `Problem` template.
- If possible, provide:
  - Application version,
  - Operating system,
  - Steps to reproduce,
  - Expected and actual behavior,
  - Log file or error text.

The more precise your description, the faster we can help.

## Local Development from Source

Python `>=3.8` is required.

```bash
pip install -e .
```

Running:

- console mode: `tg-ws-proxy`
- Windows tray: `tg-ws-proxy-tray-win`
- macOS tray: `tg-ws-proxy-tray-macos`
- Linux tray: `tg-ws-proxy-tray-linux`

Details: `docs/BuildFromSource.md`.

## Checks

Tests use the standard library only, no extra dependencies:

```bash
python -m unittest discover -s tests -t .
```

Linting (`ruff` is configured in `pyproject.toml`):

```bash
ruff check .
```

## Pull Request

Before opening a PR:

1. Make sure your change solves a specific problem.
2. Check that existing scenarios aren't broken; run the tests and the linter.
3. Update documentation if behavior or configuration changes.

Smaller and focused PRs are reviewed and accepted faster.

=== FILE: docs\EN\FakeTlsNginx.md ===
# Fake TLS + Upstream in Nginx

The domain in the `--fake-tls-domain` parameter should point to the same IP where the proxy is running.

## Example `nginx.conf` for Stream Module

```nginx
upstream mtproto {
    server 127.0.0.1:8446;
}

map $ssl_preread_server_name $sni_name {
    hostnames;
    example.com mtproto;
    # if you have xray with selfsni running:
    # sub.example.com  www;
    # default xray;
}

# upstream xray {
#     server 127.0.0.1:8443;
# }
#
# upstream www {
#     server 127.0.0.1:7443;
# }

server {
    proxy_protocol on;
    set_real_ip_from unix:;
    listen          443;
    proxy_pass      $sni_name;
    ssl_preread     on;
}
```

## Running Proxy Behind Nginx

```bash
python3 proxy/tg_ws_proxy.py \
  --port 8446 \
  --host 127.0.0.1 \
  --fake-tls-domain example.com \
  --proxy-protocol \
  --secret <32-hex-chars>
```

The connection link will be in `ee`-secret format:

```text
tg://proxy?server=your.domain.com&port=443&secret=ee<secret><domain_hex>
```

# TG WS Proxy for Docker

## Installation from Source

Enter the commands sequentially, one by one:

```bash
# Clone the repository
git clone https://github.com/Flowseal/tg-ws-proxy.git

# Navigate to the project folder
cd tg-ws-proxy

# Build the image
docker build -t tg-ws-proxy .

# Run the container
docker run -d \
  --name tg-ws-proxy \
  --restart=always \
  -p 1443:1443 \
  tg-ws-proxy:latest

# Get the connection link
docker logs tg-ws-proxy 2>&1 | grep 'tg://proxy'
```

After running the last command, you will see a link like:

```text
tg://proxy?server=172.17.0.2&port=1443&secret=dd68f127db1d...
```

## Configuring Parameters

All settings are configured using environment variables when running the container:

| Variable                | Description                                        | Default                               |
| ----------------------- | -------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------- |
| `TG_WS_PROXY_HOST`      | Address for incoming connections                   | `0.0.0.0`                             |
| `TG_WS_PROXY_PORT`      | Port inside the container                          | `1443`                                |
| `TG_WS_PROXY_SECRET`    | Secret key                                         | `random`                              |
| `TG_WS_PROXY_DC_IPS`    | DC number:IP pairs separated by space              | `2:149.154.167.220 4:149.154.167.220` |
| `TG_WS_PROXY_CF_WORKER` | Cloudflare Worker domain                           | `None`                                |
| `TG_WS_PROXY_NO_SECURE` | Use 80 port for CF-proxy and CF-worker connections | `False`                               |

Example with manually specified secret:

```bash
docker run -d \
  --name tg-ws-proxy \
  --restart=always \
  -p 1443:1443 \
  -e TG_WS_PROXY_SECRET="your_secret" \
  tg-ws-proxy:latest
```

To generate a secret, you can use:

```bash
openssl rand -hex 16  
```

## Configuring Telegram Desktop

1. Telegram в†’ **Settings** в†’ **Advanced** в†’ **Connection type** в†’ **Proxy**
2. Add proxy:
   - **Type:** MTProto
   - **Server:** `127.0.0.1` (or your custom address)
   - **Port:** `1443` (or your custom port)
   - **Secret:** from settings or logs

=== FILE: docs\EN\README.linux.md ===
# TG WS Proxy for Linux

## Prebuilt Packages

For Debian/Ubuntu, download the `TgWsProxy_linux_amd64.deb` package from the [releases page](https://github.com/Flowseal/tg-ws-proxy/releases).

For Arch and Arch-based distributions, packages are available in AUR:

- [tg-ws-proxy-bin](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/tg-ws-proxy-bin)
- [tg-ws-proxy-git](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/tg-ws-proxy-git)
- [tg-ws-proxy-cli](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/tg-ws-proxy-cli)

```shell
# Installation without AUR helper
git clone https://aur.archlinux.org/tg-ws-proxy-bin.git
cd tg-ws-proxy-bin
makepkg -si

# Using AUR helper
paru -S tg-ws-proxy-bin

# For -cli package, run via systemd (8888 вЂ” port number; secret can be generated with openssl rand -hex 16)
sudo systemctl start tg-ws-proxy@8888:3075abe65830f0325116bb0416cadf9f
```

For other distributions, you can use `TgWsProxy_linux_amd64` (binary for x86_64).

```bash
chmod +x TgWsProxy_linux_amd64
./TgWsProxy_linux_amd64
```

On first launch, a window will open with instructions. The application runs in the system tray (AppIndicator required).

## Updating

When update checks are enabled in settings, the app offers to update itself on startup and installs the new version:

- installed from `.deb`/`.rpm` вЂ” the new package is downloaded and installed by the package manager (via `pkexec`, password confirmation required);
- standalone binary вЂ” replaced in place if its directory is writable, otherwise also via `pkexec`.

The downloaded file is checked against the response length and the SHA-256 digest from the release; a corrupted download is never installed. The app restarts automatically after the update.

For AUR packages, `pip` installs, and non-x86_64 architectures the previous behaviour applies вЂ” the release page is opened.

## Configuring Telegram Desktop

1. Telegram в†’ **Settings** в†’ **Advanced** в†’ **Connection type** в†’ **Proxy**
2. Add proxy:
   - **Type:** MTProto
   - **Server:** `127.0.0.1` (or your custom address)
   - **Port:** `1443` (or your custom port)
   - **Secret:** from settings or logs

## Building from Source

Detailed instructions: [BuildFromSource.md](./BuildFromSource.md)

```bash
pip install -e .
tg-ws-proxy-tray-linux
```

# TG WS Proxy

**Local MTProto proxy** for Telegram Desktop that **speeds up Telegram**, redirecting traffic through WebSocket connections. Data is transmitted in the same encrypted form, and no external servers are needed.

<picture>
  <source srcset="../images/preview-dark.png" media="(prefers-color-scheme: dark)">
  <img src="../images/preview-white.png">
</picture>

## Windows: Quick Start

Go to the [releases page](https://github.com/Flowseal/tg-ws-proxy/releases) and download:

- `TgWsProxy_windows.exe` (Windows 10+ x64)
- `TgWsProxy_windows_arm64.exe` (Windows 10+ ARM64)
- `TgWsProxy_windows_7_64bit.exe` (Windows 7 x64)
- `TgWsProxy_windows_7_32bit.exe` (Windows 7 x32)

On first launch, a window will open with instructions for connecting Telegram Desktop. **The application minimizes to system tray.**

### Tray Menu

- **Open in Telegram** вЂ” automatically configure proxy via `tg://proxy` link
- **Copy Link** вЂ” copy the proxy connection link
- **Restart Proxy** вЂ” restart without exiting the application
- **Settings...** вЂ” GUI configuration editor (app version, optional GitHub update checks)
- **Open Logs** вЂ” open log file
- **Exit** вЂ” stop proxy and close application

### Configuring Telegram Desktop

**Automatic Setup**

Right-click the tray icon and select **"Open in Telegram"**.

If it doesn't work (Telegram doesn't open with proxy), follow these steps:

1. Right-click the tray icon and select **"Copy Link"**
2. Send the link to "Saved Messages" in Telegram and click it
3. Connect

**Manual Setup**

1. Telegram в†’ **Settings** в†’ **Advanced** в†’ **Connection type** в†’ **Proxy**
2. Add proxy:
   - **Type:** MTProto
   - **Server:** `127.0.0.1` (or your custom address)
   - **Port:** `1443` (or your custom port)
   - **Secret:** from settings or logs

## How It Works

```
Telegram Desktop в†’ MTProto Proxy (127.0.0.1:1443) в†’ WebSocket в†’ Telegram DC
```

1. Application starts MTProto proxy on `127.0.0.1:1443`
2. Intercepts connections to Telegram IP addresses
3. Extracts DC ID from MTProto obfuscation init packet
4. Establishes WebSocket connection (TLS) to corresponding DC via Telegram domains
5. If WS unavailable (302 redirect) вЂ” automatically switches to CfProxy / direct TCP connection

> [!IMPORTANT] 
> ### Photos/Videos Not Loading?
> **In proxy settings, leave only `4:149.154.167.220` in DC в†’ IP**  
> **If that doesn't work, clear the field completely**  
> This issue occurs on non-Premium accounts  
> If still not working, set up your own domain following: [CfProxy.md](./CfProxy.md)

## Automatic Build

The project contains PyInstaller specs ([`packaging/windows.spec`](../../packaging/windows.spec), [`packaging/macos.spec`](../../packaging/macos.spec), [`packaging/linux.spec`](../../packaging/linux.spec)) and GitHub Actions workflow ([`.github/workflows/build.yml`](../../.github/workflows/build.yml)) for automated builds.

Minimum supported OS versions for current binary builds:

- Windows 10+ x64 for `TgWsProxy_windows.exe`
- Windows 10+ ARM64 for `TgWsProxy_windows_arm64.exe`
- Windows 7 (x64) for `TgWsProxy_windows_7_64bit.exe`
- Windows 7 (x32) for `TgWsProxy_windows_7_32bit.exe`
- Intel macOS 10.15+
- Apple Silicon macOS 11.0+
- Linux x86_64 (AppIndicator required for system tray)

On first launch, a window will open with instructions for connecting Telegram Desktop. **The application minimizes to system tray.**

## Tray Menu

- **Open in Telegram** вЂ” automatically configure proxy via `tg://proxy` link
- **Copy Link** вЂ” copy the proxy connection link
- **Restart Proxy** вЂ” restart without exiting the application
- **Settings...** вЂ” GUI configuration editor (app version, optional GitHub update checks)
- **Open Logs** вЂ” open log file
- **Exit** вЂ” stop proxy and close application

On first launch after startup, you may be prompted to open the release page if a new version is available on GitHub (this check can be disabled in settings).

## Configuring Telegram Desktop

### Automatic Setup

Right-click the tray icon and select **"Open in Telegram"**.

If it doesn't work (Telegram doesn't open with proxy), follow these steps:

1. Right-click the tray icon and select **"Copy Link"**
2. Send the link to "Saved Messages" in Telegram and click it

   - **Type:** MTProto

=== FILE: docs\EN\TestDc.md ===
# Telegram Test Environment (Test DCs)

Traffic routing to Telegram test data centers (test environment).
Useful for developing/testing bots and clients within the Telegram test environment.

## How to Enable

**Automatically.** Telegram Desktop marks test DCs with a +10000
offset (10001вЂ“10003). The proxy automatically detects this offset вЂ” no configuration needed, allowing
you to use production and test accounts simultaneously in a single client.

**Forced.** For clients that report test DCs as standard 1-3
(Telethon, TDLib) вЂ” they cannot be detected automatically. In this case, all traffic
is forcibly routed to test DCs (production accounts will stop working through this proxy).
To force this behavior, use the `--force-test-dc` flag in CLI:

```bash
tg-ws-proxy --force-test-dc      # + your --secret / --port
```

## Limitations

Only works for **direct DC в†’ IP** and **Cloudflare Worker** routes (see [Setting up a Cloudflare Worker](./CfWorker.md)).
=== FILE: docs\EN\TrayConfig.md ===
# Tray Application Configuration Files

The tray application stores data in:

- **Windows:** `%APPDATA%/TgWsProxy`
- **macOS:** `~/Library/Application Support/TgWsProxy`
- **Linux:** `~/.config/TgWsProxy` (or `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/TgWsProxy`)

```json
{
  "host": "127.0.0.1",
  "port": 1443,
  "secret": "...",
  "dc_ip": [
    "2:149.154.167.220",
    "4:149.154.167.220"
  ],
  "verbose": false,
  "buf_kb": 256,
  "pool_size": 4,
  "log_max_mb": 5,
  "check_updates": true,
  "cfproxy": true,
  "h2": true,
  "cfproxy_user_domain_enabled": false,
  "cfproxy_user_domain": [],
  "cfproxy_worker_enabled": false,
  "cfproxy_worker_domain": [],
  "force_test_dc": false,
  "no_secure": false,
  "appearance": "auto",
  "language": "en"
}
```

The `check_updates` key: when `true`, performs a request to GitHub and compares the current version with the latest release (notification and link to download page only).  
On Windows, the config may contain `autostart` (auto-start on system login).

| `--no-cfproxy` | `false` | РћС‚РєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєСѓ [РїСЂРѕРєСЃРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёСЏ С‡РµСЂРµР· Cloudflare](./CfProxy.md) |
| `--no-h2` | `false` | РћС‚РєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ РјСѓР»СЊС‚РёРїР»РµРєСЃРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ РјРµРґРёР° С‡РµСЂРµР· HTTP/2 РІ CF-РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё |
| `--cfproxy-domain` | | РЈРєР°Р·Р°С‚СЊ СЃРІРѕР№ РґРѕРјРµРЅ РґР»СЏ РїСЂРѕРєСЃРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёСЏ С‡РµСЂРµР· Cloudflare [РџРѕРґСЂРѕР±РЅРµРµ](./CfProxy.md). РњРѕР¶РЅРѕ СѓРєР°Р·Р°С‚СЊ РЅРµСЃРєРѕР»СЊРєРѕ С‡РµСЂРµР· РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂРµРЅРёРµ Р°СЂРіСѓРјРµРЅС‚Р°. |
| `--cfproxy-worker-domain` | | Р”РѕРјРµРЅ Cloudflare Worker [РџРѕРґСЂРѕР±РЅРµРµ](./CfWorker.md). РњРѕР¶РЅРѕ СѓРєР°Р·Р°С‚СЊ РЅРµСЃРєРѕР»СЊРєРѕ С‡РµСЂРµР· РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂРµРЅРёРµ Р°СЂРіСѓРјРµРЅС‚Р°. |
| `--no-secure` | `false` | РџСЂРёРЅСѓРґРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚ 80 РїРѕСЂС‚ РґР»СЏ CF-proxy Рё CF-worker |
| `--fake-tls-domain` | | Р’РєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ РјР°СЃРєРёСЂРѕРІРєСѓ Fake TLS (ee-secret) СЃ СѓРєР°Р·Р°РЅРЅС‹Рј SNI-РґРѕРјРµРЅРѕРј |
| `--proxy-protocol` | РІС‹РєР». | РџСЂРёРЅРёРјР°С‚СЊ HAProxy PROXY protocol v1 (РґР»СЏ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚С‹ Р·Р° nginx/haproxy СЃ `proxy_protocol on`) |
| `--force-test-dc` | `false` | РќР°РїСЂР°РІР»СЏС‚СЊ РІРµСЃСЊ С‚СЂР°С„РёРє РЅР° С‚РµСЃС‚РѕРІС‹Рµ DC Telegram [РџРѕРґСЂРѕР±РЅРµРµ](./TestDc.md) |
| `--buf-kb` | `256` | Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ Р±СѓС„РµСЂР° РІ РљР‘ |
| `--pool-size` | `4` | РљРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІРѕ Р·Р°РіРѕС‚РѕРІР»РµРЅРЅС‹С… WS-СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёР№ РЅР° РєР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ DC. `0` РѕС‚РєР»СЋС‡Р°РµС‚ РїСЂСЏРјРѕР№ WS-РјР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚ DCв†’IP |
| `--log-file` | РІС‹РєР». | РџСѓС‚СЊ Рє С„Р°Р№Р»Сѓ, РІ РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Р№ Р±СѓРґСѓС‚ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏС‚СЊСЃСЏ Р»РѕРіРё |
| `--log-max-mb` | `5` | РњР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂ С„Р°Р№Р»Р° Р»РѕРіРѕРІ РІ РњР‘ (РїРѕСЃР»Рµ СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ РЅР°С‡РёРЅР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РїРµСЂРµР·Р°РїРёСЃСЊ) |
| `--log-backups` | `1` | РЎРєРѕР»СЊРєРѕ СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹С… С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ Р»РѕРіРѕРІ С…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ РїСЂРё СЂРѕС‚Р°С†РёРё (РјРёРЅРёРјСѓРј 1) |
| `-v`, `--verbose` | РІС‹РєР». | РџРѕРґСЂРѕР±РЅРѕРµ Р»РѕРіРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ (DEBUG) |

**РџСЂРёРјРµСЂС‹:**

```bash
# РЎС‚Р°РЅРґР°СЂС‚РЅС‹Р№ Р·Р°РїСѓСЃРє
tg-ws-proxy --secret 00112233445566778899aabbccddeeff

# Р”СЂСѓРіРѕР№ РїРѕСЂС‚ Рё РґРѕРїРѕР»РЅРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ DC
tg-ws-proxy --port 9050 --dc-ip 1:149.154.175.205 --dc-ip 2:149.154.167.220

# РЎ РїРѕРґСЂРѕР±РЅС‹Рј Р»РѕРіРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµРј, Р±РµР· РїСЂСЏРјРѕРіРѕ РїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЏ Рє DC
tg-ws-proxy -v --dc-ip

# Fake TLS РјР°СЃРєРёСЂРѕРІРєР° (ee-secret)
tg-ws-proxy --fake-tls-domain example.com
```

# Cloudflare-РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё

Р”Р»СЏ РЅРµРґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРЅС‹С… РґР°С‚Р°-С†РµРЅС‚СЂРѕРІ РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚СЊ Р°Р»СЊС‚РµСЂРЅР°С‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№ Р±РµСЃРїР»Р°С‚РЅС‹Р№ СЃРїРѕСЃРѕР± РїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЏ вЂ” РїСЂРѕРєСЃРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ С‡РµСЂРµР· Cloudflare. **Р”Р»СЏ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚С‹ РЅСѓР¶РµРЅ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґРѕРјРµРЅ**. Р’ РїСЂРёР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёРё РµСЃС‚СЊ РґРѕРјРµРЅ РїРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ, РЅРѕ РµРіРѕ РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ (Рё Р¶РµР»Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ) Р·Р°РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ РЅР° СЃРІРѕР№.
  
РџСЂРѕРєСЃРё РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ РґРѕСЃС‚СѓРї Рє С‚РѕРјСѓ, С‡С‚Рѕ СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ РЅРµ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР¶Р°Р»РѕСЃСЊ (СЂРµР°РєС†РёРё, РЅРµРєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рµ СЃС‚РёРєРµСЂС‹). Р•СЃР»Рё РЅР° Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚Рµ Р±РµР· Premium РЅРµ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР¶Р°СЋС‚СЃСЏ С„РѕС‚Рѕ/РІРёРґРµРѕ, РѕСЃС‚Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ РІ Р±Р»РѕРєРµ `DC в†’ IP` С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ `4:149.154.167.220`. Р•СЃР»Рё CF-РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚, РјРµРґРёР° СЃРЅРѕРІР° РЅР°С‡РЅРµС‚ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР¶Р°С‚СЊСЃСЏ.

## Р—Р°С‡РµРј РјРЅРµ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂР°РёРІР°С‚СЊ СЃРІРѕР№ РґРѕРјРµРЅ?

Cloudflare РёРјРµРµС‚ Р»РёРјРёС‚С‹ РЅР° РѕРґРЅРѕРІСЂРµРјРµРЅРЅРѕРµ РєРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІРѕ WS-РїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёР№. Р”РѕРјРµРЅ РїРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ РјРѕР¶РµС‚ РїРµСЂРµСЃС‚Р°С‚СЊ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°С‚СЊ РІ Р»СЋР±РѕР№ РјРѕРјРµРЅС‚.

## РќР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР° СЃРІРѕРµРіРѕ РґРѕРјРµРЅР°

1. Р”РѕР±Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ СЃРІРѕР№ РґРѕРјРµРЅ РІ Cloudflare (Р»РёР±Рѕ РєСѓРїРёРІ РµРіРѕ РЅР°РїСЂСЏРјСѓСЋ Сѓ Cloudflare, Р»РёР±Рѕ РёР·РјРµРЅРёРІ NS-СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂС‹: https://developers.cloudflare.com/dns/zone-setups/full-setup/setup/). Р”РѕРјРµРЅС‹ СЃС‚РѕСЏС‚ РїСЂРёРјРµСЂРЅРѕ 150 СЂСѓР±Р»РµР№ РІ РіРѕРґ, РїРѕРґРѕР№РґС‘С‚ Р»СЋР±РѕР№.

2. Р’ `SSL/TLS` в†’ `Overview` РІС‹СЃС‚Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ СЂРµР¶РёРј **Flexible**.

3. Р’ `DNS` в†’ `Records` РґРѕР±Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РёРµ `A`-Р·Р°РїРёСЃРё С‡РµСЂРµР· `+ Add Record`:
- Name=`kws1`   IPv4=`149.154.175.50`
- Name=`kws2`   IPv4=`149.154.167.51`
- Name=`kws3`   IPv4=`149.154.175.100`
- Name=`kws4`   IPv4=`149.154.167.91`
- Name=`kws5`   IPv4=`149.154.171.5`
- Name=`kws203` IPv4=`91.105.192.100`

4. **Р”РѕР±Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ РґРѕРјРµРЅ РІ [zapret](https://github.com/Flowseal/zapret-discord-youtube/) РёР»Рё РІ Р»СЋР±РѕРµ РґСЂСѓРіРѕРµ РџРћ, С‚Р°Рє РєР°Рє РїРѕРґСЃРµС‚СЊ Cloudflare РјРѕР¶РµС‚ Р±С‹С‚СЊ Р·Р°Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅР° (РЅР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ, РІ Р РѕСЃСЃРёРё).**

5. Р’ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР°С… `TgWsProxy` Р·Р°РјРµРЅРёС‚Рµ РґРѕРјРµРЅ РЅР° СЃРІРѕР№.

## РњСѓР»СЊС‚РёРїР»РµРєСЃРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ РјРµРґРёР° С‡РµСЂРµР· HTTP/2

РњСѓР»СЊС‚РёРїР»РµРєСЃРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ РІРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРѕ РїРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ. РњРµРґРёР°-СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёСЏ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓСЋС‚ `/api` РІРјРµСЃС‚Рѕ `/apiws` С‡РµСЂРµР· РѕРґРЅРѕ РѕР±С‰РµРµ TCP/TLS-СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёРµ РґРѕ Cloudflare РґР»СЏ РєР°Р¶РґРѕРіРѕ DC Рё РґРѕРјРµРЅР°. РћСЃРЅРѕРІРЅС‹Рµ СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёСЏ Рё С‚РµСЃС‚РѕРІС‹Рµ DC РїСЂРѕРґРѕР»Р¶Р°СЋС‚ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚СЊ WS.  
Р§С‚РѕР±С‹ РѕС‚РєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ РјСѓР»СЊС‚РёРїР»РµРєСЃРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ, РґРѕР±Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ `--no-h2` РёР»Рё СЃРЅРёРјРёС‚Рµ РіР°Р»РѕС‡РєСѓ
**РњСѓР»СЊС‚РёРїР»РµРєСЃРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ РјРµРґРёР° (HTTP/2)** РІ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР°С… **Cloudflare Proxy**.

## Р‘Р»Р°РіРѕРґР°СЂРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё

- РРґРµСЏ: https://github.com/Nekogram/WSProxy
- РЎРїР°СЃРёР±Рѕ [@UjuiUjuMandan](https://github.com/UjuiUjuMandan) Р·Р° РёРЅС„РѕСЂРјР°С†РёСЋ.

# Cloudflare Worker

РђР»СЊС‚РµСЂРЅР°С‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№ (РїРѕР»РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊСЋ Р±РµСЃРїР»Р°С‚РЅС‹Р№, РЅРµ РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ РїРѕРєСѓРїР°С‚СЊ РґРѕРјРµРЅ РІ РѕС‚Р»РёС‡РёРё РѕС‚ [CfProxy](./CfProxy.md)) СЃРїРѕСЃРѕР± РїСЂРѕРєСЃРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёСЏ.

РџСЂРѕРєСЃРё РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ РґРѕСЃС‚СѓРї Рє С‚РѕРјСѓ, С‡С‚Рѕ СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ РЅРµ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР¶Р°Р»РѕСЃСЊ (СЂРµР°РєС†РёРё, РЅРµРєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рµ СЃС‚РёРєРµСЂС‹). Р•СЃР»Рё РЅР° Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚Рµ Р±РµР· Premium СЃ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рј СЃРїРѕСЃРѕР±РѕРј РІСЃРµ РµС‰Рµ РЅРµ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР¶Р°СЋС‚СЃСЏ С„РѕС‚Рѕ/РІРёРґРµРѕ, РѕСЃС‚Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ РІ Р±Р»РѕРєРµ `DC в†’ IP` С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ `4:149.154.167.220`

##

1. **Р”РѕР±Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ РІ [zapret](https://github.com/Flowseal/zapret-discord-youtube/) РёР»Рё РІ Р»СЋР±РѕРµ РґСЂСѓРіРѕРµ РџРћ СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РёРµ РґРѕРјРµРЅС‹:**
```
cloudflare.com
cloudflare.dev
workers.dev
```
2. РЎРѕР·РґР°Р№С‚Рµ Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚ РІ [Cloudflare](https://dash.cloudflare.com/) (РёР»Рё РІРѕР№РґРёС‚Рµ РІ СЃСѓС‰РµСЃС‚РІСѓСЋС‰РёР№)
	* **РџРѕСЃР»Рµ СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅРёСЏ Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚Р° РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂРґРёС‚Рµ РїРѕС‡С‚Сѓ СЃ РїРѕРјРѕС‰СЊСЋ РїРёСЃСЊРјР°, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Р№ РІР°Рј РїСЂРёС€РµР» РЅР° email**
3. РЎР»РµРІР° РІ РїР°РЅРµР»Рё РІС‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ `Compute` в†’ `Workers & Pages`

    * Р•СЃР»Рё Сѓ РІР°СЃ РЅРµ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР¶Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ РєРѕРґ, С‚Рѕ РІС‹ РЅРµ РІС‹РїРѕР»РЅРёР»Рё РїРµСЂРІС‹Р№ РїСѓРЅРєС‚

6. РќР°Р¶РјРёС‚Рµ СЃРІРµСЂС…Сѓ СЃРїСЂР°РІР° РєРЅРѕРїРєСѓ **`Deploy`**

7. РЎРєРѕРїРёСЂСѓР№С‚Рµ РґРѕРјРµРЅ РёР· РїРѕР»СЏ СЃРїСЂР°РІР° Рё СѓРєР°Р¶РёС‚Рµ РµРіРѕ РІ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР°С… **Cloudflare Worker** (РёР»Рё С‡РµСЂРµР· Р°СЂРіСѓРјРµРЅС‚ `--cfproxy-worker-domain`)
    * РџСЂРёРјРµСЂ РґРѕРјРµРЅР°: `random-symbols-1234.username.workers.dev`
	* **РњРѕР¶РЅРѕ СѓРєР°Р·С‹РІР°С‚СЊ РЅРµСЃРєРѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґРѕРјРµРЅРѕРІ С‡РµСЂРµР· Р·Р°РїСЏС‚СѓСЋ (РёР»Рё РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂРµРЅРёРµРј Р°СЂРіСѓРјРµРЅС‚Р° `--cfproxy-worker-domain`)**
   <img width="414" height="182" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/4fb0b111-8026-4d17-b993-6c70ec37f1f5" />


### РљРѕРґ Worker'Р°
```javascript
import { connect } from "cloudflare:sockets";

function toBytes(data) {
	if (data instanceof ArrayBuffer) {
		return new Uint8Array(data);
	}
	if (typeof data === "string") {
		return new TextEncoder().encode(data);
	}
	if (data && typeof data.arrayBuffer === "function") {
		return data.arrayBuffer().then((ab) => new Uint8Array(ab));
	}
	return new Uint8Array();
}

export default {
	async fetch(request) {
		if ((request.headers.get("Upgrade") || "").toLowerCase() !== "websocket") {
			return new Response("Expected websocket", { status: 426 });
		}

		const url = new URL(request.url);
		if (url.pathname !== "/apiws") {
			return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
		}

		const dst = url.searchParams.get("dst");
		const pair = new WebSocketPair();
		const client = pair[0];
		const server = pair[1];
		server.accept();

		const socket = connect({ hostname: dst, port: 443 });
		const tcpReader = socket.readable.getReader();
		const tcpWriter = socket.writable.getWriter();

		server.addEventListener("message", async (event) => {
			try {
				await tcpWriter.write(await toBytes(event.data));
			} catch {
				try {
					server.close(1011, "tcp write failed");
				} catch {}
			}
		});

		server.addEventListener("close", async () => {
			try {
				await tcpWriter.close();
			} catch {}
			try {
				socket.close();
			} catch {}
		});

		(async () => {
			try {
				while (true) {
					const { value, done } = await tcpReader.read();
					if (done) {
						break;
					}
					if (value) {
						server.send(value);
					}
				}
			} catch {
			} finally {
				try {
					server.close();
				} catch {}
				try {
					tcpReader.releaseLock();
				} catch {}
				try {
					socket.close();
				} catch {}
			}
		})();

		return new Response(null, { status: 101, webSocket: client });
	},
};
```

=== FILE: docs\RU\FakeTlsNginx.md ===
# Fake TLS + upstream РІ nginx

Р”РѕРјРµРЅ РІ РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂРµ `--fake-tls-domain` РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ СѓРєР°Р·С‹РІР°С‚СЊ РЅР° С‚РѕС‚ Р¶Рµ IP, РЅР° РєРѕС‚РѕСЂРѕРј Р·Р°РїСѓС‰РµРЅ РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё.

## РџСЂРёРјРµСЂ `nginx.conf` РґР»СЏ stream-РјРѕРґСѓР»СЏ

```nginx
upstream mtproto {
    server 127.0.0.1:8446;
}

map $ssl_preread_server_name $sni_name {
    hostnames;
    example.com mtproto;
    # if you have xray with selfsni running:
    # sub.example.com  www;
    # default xray;
}

# upstream xray {
#     server 127.0.0.1:8443;
# }
#
# upstream www {
#     server 127.0.0.1:7443;
# }

server {
    proxy_protocol on;
    set_real_ip_from unix:;
    listen          443;
    proxy_pass      $sni_name;
    ssl_preread     on;
}
```

## Р—Р°РїСѓСЃРє РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё Р·Р° Nginx

```bash
python3 proxy/tg_ws_proxy.py \
  --port 8446 \
  --host 127.0.0.1 \
  --fake-tls-domain example.com \
  --proxy-protocol \
  --secret <32-hex-chars>
```

РЎСЃС‹Р»РєР° РґР»СЏ РїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЏ Р±СѓРґРµС‚ РІ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚Рµ `ee`-СЃРµРєСЂРµС‚Р°:

```text
tg://proxy?server=your.domain.com&port=443&secret=ee<secret><domain_hex>
```

# TG WS Proxy РґР»СЏ Docker

## РЈСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєР° РёР· РёСЃС…РѕРґРЅРёРєРѕРІ

Р’РІРѕРґРёС‚Рµ РєРѕРјР°РЅРґС‹ РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ, РѕРґРЅСѓ Р·Р° РґСЂСѓРіРѕР№:

```bash
# РЎРєР°С‡РёРІР°РµРј СЂРµРїРѕР·РёС‚РѕСЂРёР№
git clone https://github.com/Flowseal/tg-ws-proxy.git

# РџРµСЂРµС…РѕРґРёРј РІ РїР°РїРєСѓ СЃ РїСЂРѕРµРєС‚РѕРј
cd tg-ws-proxy

# РЎРѕР±РёСЂР°РµРј РѕР±СЂР°Р·
docker build -t tg-ws-proxy .

# Р—Р°РїСѓСЃРєР°РµРј РєРѕРЅС‚РµР№РЅРµСЂ
docker run -d \
  --name tg-ws-proxy \
  --restart=always \
  -p 1443:1443 \
  tg-ws-proxy:latest

# РџРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј СЃСЃС‹Р»РєСѓ РґР»СЏ РїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЏ

```

РџРѕСЃР»Рµ РІС‹РїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРёСЏ РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРµР№ РєРѕРјР°РЅРґС‹ РІС‹ СѓРІРёРґРёС‚Рµ СЃСЃС‹Р»РєСѓ РІРёРґР°:

## РќР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР° РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂРѕРІ

Р’СЃРµ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё Р·Р°РґР°СЋС‚СЃСЏ РїРµСЂРµРјРµРЅРЅС‹РјРё РѕРєСЂСѓР¶РµРЅРёСЏ РїСЂРё Р·Р°РїСѓСЃРєРµ РєРѕРЅС‚РµР№РЅРµСЂР°:

| РџРµСЂРµРјРµРЅРЅР°СЏ              | РћРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµ                                                      | РџРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ                          |
| ----------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------- |
| `TG_WS_PROXY_HOST`      | `РђРґСЂРµСЃ РґР»СЏ РїСЂРёС‘РјР° РїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёР№`                                | `0.0.0.0`                             |
| `TG_WS_PROXY_PORT`      | `РџРѕСЂС‚ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё РєРѕРЅС‚РµР№РЅРµСЂР°`                                      | `1443`                                |
| `TG_WS_PROXY_SECRET`    | `РЎРµРєСЂРµС‚РЅС‹Р№ РєР»СЋС‡`                                              | `random`                              |
| `TG_WS_PROXY_DC_IPS`    | `РџР°СЂС‹ В«РЅРѕРјРµСЂ DC:IPВ» С‡РµСЂРµР· РїСЂРѕР±РµР»`                             | `2:149.154.167.220 4:149.154.167.220` |
| `TG_WS_PROXY_CF_WORKER` | `Р”РѕРјРµРЅ Cloudflare Worker`                                     | `None`                                |
| `TG_WS_PROXY_NO_SECURE` | `РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚СЊ 80 РїРѕСЂС‚ РґР»СЏ РїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЏ Рє CF-proxy Рё CF-Worker` | `False`                               |

РџСЂРёРјРµСЂ СЃ СЂСѓС‡РЅС‹Рј СѓРєР°Р·Р°РЅРёРµРј СЃРµРєСЂРµС‚Р°:

```bash
docker run -d \
  --name tg-ws-proxy \
  --restart=always \
  -p 1443:1443 \
  -e TG_WS_PROXY_SECRET="РІР°С€_СЃРµРєСЂРµС‚" \
  tg-ws-proxy:latest
```

Р”Р»СЏ РіРµРЅРµСЂР°С†РёРё СЃРµРєСЂРµС‚Р° РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚СЊ:

# TG WS Proxy РґР»СЏ Linux

## Р“РѕС‚РѕРІС‹Рµ СЃР±РѕСЂРєРё

Р”Р»СЏ Debian/Ubuntu СЃРєР°С‡Р°Р№С‚Рµ СЃРѕ [СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ СЂРµР»РёР·РѕРІ](https://github.com/Flowseal/tg-ws-proxy/releases) РїР°РєРµС‚ `TgWsProxy_linux_amd64.deb`.

Р”Р»СЏ Arch Рё РѕСЃРЅРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹С… РЅР° Arch РґРёСЃС‚СЂРёР±СѓС‚РёРІРѕРІ РїРѕРґРіРѕС‚РѕРІР»РµРЅС‹ РїР°РєРµС‚С‹ РІ AUR:

- [tg-ws-proxy-bin](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/tg-ws-proxy-bin)
- [tg-ws-proxy-git](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/tg-ws-proxy-git)
- [tg-ws-proxy-cli](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/tg-ws-proxy-cli)

```shell
# РЈСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєР° Р±РµР· AUR-helper
git clone https://aur.archlinux.org/tg-ws-proxy-bin.git
cd tg-ws-proxy-bin
makepkg -si

# РџСЂРё РїРѕРјРѕС‰Рё AUR-helper
paru -S tg-ws-proxy-bin

# Р”Р»СЏ РїР°РєРµС‚Р° -cli Р·Р°РїСѓСЃРє С‡РµСЂРµР· systemd (8888 вЂ” РЅРѕРјРµСЂ РїРѕСЂС‚Р°; secret РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ СЃРіРµРЅРµСЂРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ РєРѕРјР°РЅРґРѕР№ openssl rand -hex 16)
sudo systemctl start tg-ws-proxy@8888:3075abe65830f0325116bb0416cadf9f
```

Р”Р»СЏ РѕСЃС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… РґРёСЃС‚СЂРёР±СѓС‚РёРІРѕРІ РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚СЊ `TgWsProxy_linux_amd64` (Р±РёРЅР°СЂРЅС‹Р№ С„Р°Р№Р» РґР»СЏ x86_64).

```bash
chmod +x TgWsProxy_linux_amd64
./TgWsProxy_linux_amd64
```

РџСЂРё РїРµСЂРІРѕРј Р·Р°РїСѓСЃРєРµ РѕС‚РєСЂРѕРµС‚СЃСЏ РѕРєРЅРѕ СЃ РёРЅСЃС‚СЂСѓРєС†РёРµР№. РџСЂРёР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёРµ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ РІ СЃРёСЃС‚РµРјРЅРѕРј С‚СЂРµРµ (С‚СЂРµР±СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ AppIndicator).

## РћР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРµ

Р•СЃР»Рё РІ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР°С… РІРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅР° РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёР№, РїСЂРё Р·Р°РїСѓСЃРєРµ РїСЂРёР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёРµ СЃР°РјРѕ РїСЂРµРґР»РѕР¶РёС‚ РѕР±РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊСЃСЏ Рё СѓСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРёС‚ РЅРѕРІСѓСЋ РІРµСЂСЃРёСЋ:

- СѓСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєР° РёР· `.deb`/`.rpm` вЂ” СЃРєР°С‡РёРІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ РїР°РєРµС‚ Рё СЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЃСЏ РїР°РєРµС‚РЅС‹Рј РјРµРЅРµРґР¶РµСЂРѕРј (С‡РµСЂРµР· `pkexec`, РїРѕС‚СЂРµР±СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅРёРµ РїР°СЂРѕР»СЏ);
- РѕС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ Р±РёРЅР°СЂРЅС‹Р№ С„Р°Р№Р» вЂ” Р·Р°РјРµРЅСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ РЅР° РјРµСЃС‚Рµ, РµСЃР»Рё РєР°С‚Р°Р»РѕРі РґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРµРЅ РЅР° Р·Р°РїРёСЃСЊ, РёРЅР°С‡Рµ С‚РѕР¶Рµ С‡РµСЂРµР· `pkexec`.

Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР¶РµРЅРЅС‹Р№ С„Р°Р№Р» РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ РїРѕ РґР»РёРЅРµ РѕС‚РІРµС‚Р° Рё РєРѕРЅС‚СЂРѕР»СЊРЅРѕР№ СЃСѓРјРјРµ SHA-256 РёР· СЂРµР»РёР·Р°, РїРѕРІСЂРµР¶РґС‘РЅРЅР°СЏ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° РЅРµ СѓСЃС‚Р°РЅР°РІР»РёРІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ. РџРѕСЃР»Рµ СѓСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєРё РїСЂРёР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёРµ РїРµСЂРµР·Р°РїСѓСЃРєР°РµС‚СЃСЏ Р°РІС‚РѕРјР°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРё.

Р”Р»СЏ РїР°РєРµС‚РѕРІ РёР· AUR, СѓСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєРё С‡РµСЂРµР· `pip` Рё Р°СЂС…РёС‚РµРєС‚СѓСЂ, РѕС‚Р»РёС‡РЅС‹С… РѕС‚ x86_64, РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРµ РІС‹РїРѕР»РЅСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ РїСЂРµР¶РЅРёРј СЃРїРѕСЃРѕР±РѕРј вЂ” РѕС‚РєСЂС‹РІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° СЂРµР»РёР·Р°.

## РќР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР° Telegram Desktop

1. Telegram в†’ **РќР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё** в†’ **РџСЂРѕРґРІРёРЅСѓС‚С‹Рµ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё** в†’ **РўРёРї РїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЏ** в†’ **РџСЂРѕРєСЃРё**
2. Р”РѕР±Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё:
   - **РўРёРї:** MTProto
   - **РЎРµСЂРІРµСЂ:** `127.0.0.1` (РёР»Рё РїРµСЂРµРѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»РµРЅРЅС‹Р№ РІР°РјРё)
   - **РџРѕСЂС‚:** `1443` (РёР»Рё РїРµСЂРµРѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»РµРЅРЅС‹Р№ РІР°РјРё)
   - **Secret:** РёР· РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕРµРє РёР»Рё Р»РѕРіРѕРІ

=== FILE: docs\RU\README.macos.md ===
# TG WS Proxy РґР»СЏ macOS

РџРµСЂРµР№РґРёС‚Рµ РЅР° [СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Сѓ СЂРµР»РёР·РѕРІ](https://github.com/Flowseal/tg-ws-proxy/releases) Рё СЃРєР°С‡Р°Р№С‚Рµ `TgWsProxy_macos_universal.dmg` (СѓРЅРёРІРµСЂСЃР°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ СЃР±РѕСЂРєР° РґР»СЏ Apple Silicon Рё Intel).

1. РћС‚РєСЂРѕР№С‚Рµ РѕР±СЂР°Р·
2. РџРµСЂРµРЅРµСЃРёС‚Рµ `TG WS Proxy.app` РІ РїР°РїРєСѓ `Applications`
3. РџСЂРё РїРµСЂРІРѕРј Р·Р°РїСѓСЃРєРµ macOS РјРѕР¶РµС‚ РїРѕРїСЂРѕСЃРёС‚СЊ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂРґРёС‚СЊ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ: **РЎРёСЃС‚РµРјРЅС‹Рµ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё в†’ РљРѕРЅС„РёРґРµРЅС†РёР°Р»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ Рё Р±РµР·РѕРїР°СЃРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ в†’ Р’СЃС‘ СЂР°РІРЅРѕ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ**

РњРёРЅРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ РїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶РёРІР°РµРјС‹Рµ РІРµСЂСЃРёРё:

- Intel macOS 10.15+
- Apple Silicon macOS 11.0+

## РќР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР° Telegram Desktop

1. Telegram в†’ **РќР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё** в†’ **РџСЂРѕРґРІРёРЅСѓС‚С‹Рµ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё** в†’ **РўРёРї РїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЏ** в†’ **РџСЂРѕРєСЃРё**
2. Р”РѕР±Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё:
   - **РўРёРї:** MTProto
   - **РЎРµСЂРІРµСЂ:** `127.0.0.1` (РёР»Рё РїРµСЂРµРѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»РµРЅРЅС‹Р№ РІР°РјРё)
   - **РџРѕСЂС‚:** `1443` (РёР»Рё РїРµСЂРµРѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»РµРЅРЅС‹Р№ РІР°РјРё)
   - **Secret:** РёР· РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕРµРє РёР»Рё Р»РѕРіРѕРІ

## РЈСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєР° РёР· РёСЃС…РѕРґРЅРёРєРѕРІ

РџРѕРґСЂРѕР±РЅР°СЏ РёРЅСЃС‚СЂСѓРєС†РёСЏ: [BuildFromSource.md](./BuildFromSource.md)

Р”Р»СЏ РёРЅС‚РµСЂС„РµР№СЃР° С‚СЂРµР±СѓСЋС‚СЃСЏ Tk, CustomTkinter Рё РґРѕСЃС‚СѓРї Рє Cocoa С‡РµСЂРµР· PyObjC. РћРЅРё СѓСЃС‚Р°РЅР°РІР»РёРІР°СЋС‚СЃСЏ Р°РІС‚РѕРјР°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРё, РєСЂРѕРјРµ Tk, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Р№ РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ РІС…РѕРґРёС‚СЊ РІ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРјСѓСЋ СЃР±РѕСЂРєСѓ Python.

```bash
pip install -e .
tg-ws-proxy-tray-macos
```

# TG WS Proxy РґР»СЏ Windows

РџРµСЂРµР№РґРёС‚Рµ РЅР° [СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Сѓ СЂРµР»РёР·РѕРІ](https://github.com/Flowseal/tg-ws-proxy/releases) Рё СЃРєР°С‡Р°Р№С‚Рµ:

- `TgWsProxy_windows.exe` (Windows 10+ x64)
- `TgWsProxy_windows_arm64.exe` (Windows 10+ ARM64)
- `TgWsProxy_windows_7_64bit.exe` (Windows 7 x64)
- `TgWsProxy_windows_7_32bit.exe` (Windows 7 x32)

РЎР±РѕСЂРєРё РїСѓР±Р»РёРєСѓСЋС‚СЃСЏ Р°РІС‚РѕРјР°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРё С‡РµСЂРµР· [GitHub Actions](https://github.com/Flowseal/tg-ws-proxy/actions) РёР· РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚РѕРіРѕ РёСЃС…РѕРґРЅРѕРіРѕ РєРѕРґР°.

РџСЂРё РїРµСЂРІРѕРј Р·Р°РїСѓСЃРєРµ РѕС‚РєСЂРѕРµС‚СЃСЏ РѕРєРЅРѕ СЃ РёРЅСЃС‚СЂСѓРєС†РёРµР№ РїРѕ РїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЋ Telegram Desktop. **РџСЂРёР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёРµ СЃРІРѕСЂР°С‡РёРІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РІ СЃРёСЃС‚РµРјРЅС‹Р№ С‚СЂРµР№.**

## РњРµРЅСЋ С‚СЂРµСЏ

- **РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РІ Telegram** вЂ” Р°РІС‚РѕРјР°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРё РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕРёС‚СЊ РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё С‡РµСЂРµР· СЃСЃС‹Р»РєСѓ `tg://proxy`
- **РЎРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ СЃСЃС‹Р»РєСѓ** вЂ” СЃРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ СЃСЃС‹Р»РєСѓ РґР»СЏ РїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЏ
- **РџРµСЂРµР·Р°РїСѓСЃС‚РёС‚СЊ РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё** вЂ” РїРµСЂРµР·Р°РїСѓСЃРє Р±РµР· РІС‹С…РѕРґР° РёР· РїСЂРёР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёСЏ
- **РќР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё...** вЂ” GUI-СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ РєРѕРЅС„РёРіСѓСЂР°С†РёРё (РІРµСЂСЃРёСЏ РїСЂРёР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёСЏ, РѕРїС†РёРѕРЅР°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёР№ СЃ GitHub)
- **РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ Р»РѕРіРё** вЂ” РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ С„Р°Р№Р» Р»РѕРіРѕРІ
- **Р’С‹С…РѕРґ** вЂ” РѕСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊ РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё Рё Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РїСЂРёР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёРµ

РџСЂРё РїРµСЂРІРѕРј Р·Р°РїСѓСЃРєРµ РїРѕСЃР»Рµ СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚Р° РјРѕР¶РµС‚ РїРѕСЏРІРёС‚СЊСЃСЏ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ РѕР± РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚РёРё СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ СЂРµР»РёР·Р°, РµСЃР»Рё РЅР° GitHub РІС‹С€Р»Р° РЅРѕРІР°СЏ РІРµСЂСЃРёСЏ (СЌС‚Сѓ РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєСѓ РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ РѕС‚РєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ РІ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР°С…).

## РќР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР° Telegram Desktop

### РђРІС‚РѕРјР°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєР°СЏ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР°

Р©РµР»РєРЅРёС‚Рµ РїСЂР°РІРѕР№ РєРЅРѕРїРєРѕР№ РјС‹С€Рё РїРѕ Р·РЅР°С‡РєСѓ РІ С‚СЂРµРµ Рё РІС‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ **В«РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РІ TelegramВ»**.

Р•СЃР»Рё РЅРµ СЃСЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°Р»Рѕ (Telegram РЅРµ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹Р»СЃСЏ СЃ РїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёРµРј), РІС‹РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ С€Р°РіРё РЅРёР¶Рµ:

1. Р©РµР»РєРЅРёС‚Рµ РїСЂР°РІРѕР№ РєРЅРѕРїРєРѕР№ РјС‹С€Рё РїРѕ Р·РЅР°С‡РєСѓ РІ С‚СЂРµРµ Рё РІС‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ **В«РЎРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ СЃСЃС‹Р»РєСѓВ»**
2. РћС‚РїСЂР°РІСЊС‚Рµ СЃСЃС‹Р»РєСѓ РІ В«РР·Р±СЂР°РЅРЅРѕРµВ» РІ Telegram Рё РЅР°Р¶РјРёС‚Рµ РїРѕ РЅРµР№ Р»РµРІРѕР№ РєРЅРѕРїРєРѕР№ РјС‹С€Рё
3. РџРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РёС‚РµСЃСЊ

### Р СѓС‡РЅР°СЏ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР°

1. Telegram в†’ **РќР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё** в†’ **РџСЂРѕРґРІРёРЅСѓС‚С‹Рµ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё** в†’ **РўРёРї РїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЏ** в†’ **РџСЂРѕРєСЃРё**
2. Р”РѕР±Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё:
   - **РўРёРї:** MTProto
   - **РЎРµСЂРІРµСЂ:** `127.0.0.1` (РёР»Рё РїРµСЂРµРѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»РµРЅРЅС‹Р№ РІР°РјРё)
   - **РџРѕСЂС‚:** `1443` (РёР»Рё РїРµСЂРµРѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»РµРЅРЅС‹Р№ РІР°РјРё)
   - **Secret:** РёР· РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕРµРє РёР»Рё Р»РѕРіРѕРІ

## РџРѕСЂС‚Р°С‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№ СЂРµР¶РёРј
РџРѕСЂС‚Р°С‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№ СЂРµР¶РёРј Р°РІС‚РѕРјР°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРё РІРєР»СЋС‡Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ, РµСЃР»Рё СЂСЏРґРѕРј СЃ РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅСЏРµРјС‹Рј С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРј РµСЃС‚СЊ РїР°РїРєР° СЃ РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµРј `TgWsProxy_data`.  
Р›РёР±Рѕ РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ РїСЂРёРЅСѓРґРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ РІРєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ РїРѕСЂС‚Р°С‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№ СЂРµР¶РёРј (РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Р№ СЃР°Рј СЃРѕР·РґР°СЃС‚ РїР°РїРєСѓ), Р·Р°РїСѓСЃС‚РёРІ РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅСЏРµРјС‹Р№ С„Р°Р№Р» СЃ РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂРѕРј `--portable`.

# РўРµСЃС‚РѕРІРѕРµ РѕРєСЂСѓР¶РµРЅРёРµ Telegram (С‚РµСЃС‚РѕРІС‹Рµ DC)

РњР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ С‚СЂР°С„РёРєР° Рє С‚РµСЃС‚РѕРІС‹Рј РґР°С‚Р°-С†РµРЅС‚СЂР°Рј Telegram (test environment).
РќСѓР¶РЅР° РґР»СЏ СЂР°Р·СЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєРё/С‚РµСЃС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёСЏ Р±РѕС‚РѕРІ Рё РєР»РёРµРЅС‚РѕРІ РІ С‚РµСЃС‚РѕРІРѕР№ СЃСЂРµРґРµ Telegram.

## РљР°Рє РІРєР»СЋС‡Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ

**РђРІС‚РѕРјР°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРё.** Telegram Desktop РїРѕРјРµС‡Р°РµС‚ С‚РµСЃС‚РѕРІС‹Рµ DC СЃРґРІРёРіРѕРј +10000
(10001вЂ“10003). РџСЂРѕРєСЃРё СЂР°СЃРїРѕР·РЅР°С‘С‚ СЃРґРІРёРі СЃР°Рј вЂ” РІРєР»СЋС‡Р°С‚СЊ РЅРёС‡РµРіРѕ РЅРµ РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ, РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ
РѕРґРЅРѕРІСЂРµРјРµРЅРЅРѕ РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚СЊСЃСЏ РїСЂРѕРґРѕРІС‹Рј Рё С‚РµСЃС‚РѕРІС‹Рј Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚РѕРј РІ РѕРґРЅРѕРј РєР»РёРµРЅС‚Рµ.

**РџСЂРёРЅСѓРґРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ.** Р”Р»СЏ РєР»РёРµРЅС‚РѕРІ, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рµ СЃРѕРѕР±С‰Р°СЋС‚ С‚РµСЃС‚РѕРІС‹Рµ DC РєР°Рє РѕР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Рµ 1-3
(Telethon, TDLib) вЂ” СЂР°СЃРїРѕР·РЅР°С‚СЊ РёС… Р°РІС‚РѕРјР°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРё РЅРµР»СЊР·СЏ. РўРѕРіРґР° РІРµСЃСЊ С‚СЂР°С„РёРє
РїСЂРёРЅСѓРґРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»СЏРµС‚СЃСЏ РЅР° С‚РµСЃС‚РѕРІС‹Рµ DC (РїСЂРѕРґРѕРІС‹Рµ Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚С‹ С‡РµСЂРµР· СЌС‚РѕС‚ РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё
СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°С‚СЊ РїРµСЂРµСЃС‚Р°РЅСѓС‚). Р”Р»СЏ РїСЂРёРЅСѓРґРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕР№ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚С‹ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓР№С‚Рµ С„Р»Р°Рі `--force-test-dc` РІ CLI:

```bash
tg-ws-proxy --force-test-dc      # + РІР°С€Рё --secret / --port
```

## РћРіСЂР°РЅРёС‡РµРЅРёСЏ

Р Р°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ РјР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚РѕРІ **РїСЂСЏРјРѕР№ DC в†’ IP** Рё **Cloudflare Worker** (СЃРј. [РќР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР° Cloudflare Worker'Р°](./CfWorker.md)).
=== FILE: docs\RU\TrayConfig.md ===
# Р¤Р°Р№Р»С‹ РєРѕРЅС„РёРіСѓСЂР°С†РёРё Tray-РїСЂРёР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёСЏ

Tray-РїСЂРёР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёРµ С…СЂР°РЅРёС‚ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РІ:

- **Windows:** `%APPDATA%/TgWsProxy`
- **macOS:** `~/Library/Application Support/TgWsProxy`
- **Linux:** `~/.config/TgWsProxy` (РёР»Рё `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/TgWsProxy`)

```json
{
  "host": "127.0.0.1",
  "port": 1443,
  "secret": "...",
  "dc_ip": [
    "2:149.154.167.220",
    "4:149.154.167.220"
  ],
  "verbose": false,
  "buf_kb": 256,
  "pool_size": 4,
  "log_max_mb": 5,
  "check_updates": true,
  "cfproxy": true,
  "h2": true,
  "cfproxy_user_domain_enabled": false,
  "cfproxy_user_domain": [],
  "cfproxy_worker_enabled": false,
  "cfproxy_worker_domain": [],
  "force_test_dc": false,
  "no_secure": false,
  "appearance": "auto",
  "language": "ru"
}
```

РљР»СЋС‡ `check_updates`: РїСЂРё `true` РІС‹РїРѕР»РЅСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ Рє GitHub Рё СЃСЂР°РІРЅРµРЅРёРµ С‚РµРєСѓС‰РµР№ РІРµСЂСЃРёРё СЃ РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРёРј СЂРµР»РёР·РѕРј (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ СѓРІРµРґРѕРјР»РµРЅРёРµ Рё СЃСЃС‹Р»РєР° РЅР° СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Сѓ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё).  
РќР° Windows РІ РєРѕРЅС„РёРіРµ РјРѕР¶РµС‚ Р±С‹С‚СЊ `autostart` (Р°РІС‚РѕР·Р°РїСѓСЃРє РїСЂРё РІС…РѕРґРµ РІ СЃРёСЃС‚РµРјСѓ).

# CONTRIBUTING

РЎРїР°СЃРёР±Рѕ Р·Р° Р¶РµР»Р°РЅРёРµ РїРѕРјРѕС‡СЊ РїСЂРѕРµРєС‚Сѓ `tg-ws-proxy`.

## РџРµСЂРµРґ СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅРёРµРј issue

1. РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЊС‚Рµ РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚Р°С†РёСЋ РІ `docs/README.md`.
2. РЈР±РµРґРёС‚РµСЃСЊ, С‡С‚Рѕ РїРѕС…РѕР¶РёР№ issue РµС‰Рµ РЅРµ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚.
3. Р”Р»СЏ РєРѕСЂСЂРµРєС‚РЅРѕР№ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚С‹ triage РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓР№С‚Рµ СЃС‚Р°РЅРґР°СЂС‚РЅС‹Рµ Р»РµР№Р±Р»С‹ РёР· `.github/labels.md`.

## РљР°Рє СЃРѕРѕР±С‰Р°С‚СЊ Рѕ РїСЂРѕР±Р»РµРјР°С…

- РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓР№С‚Рµ С€Р°Р±Р»РѕРЅ `РџСЂРѕР±Р»РµРјР°`.
- РџРѕ РІРѕР·РјРѕР¶РЅРѕСЃС‚Рё СѓРєР°Р¶РёС‚Рµ:
  - РІРµСЂСЃРёСЋ РїСЂРёР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёСЏ,
  - РћРЎ,
  - С€Р°РіРё РІРѕСЃРїСЂРѕРёР·РІРµРґРµРЅРёСЏ,
  - РѕР¶РёРґР°РµРјРѕРµ Рё С„Р°РєС‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРѕРµ РїРѕРІРµРґРµРЅРёРµ,
  - Р»РѕРі-С„Р°Р№Р» РёР»Рё С‚РµРєСЃС‚ РѕС€РёР±РєРё.

## Р›РѕРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ Р·Р°РїСѓСЃРє РёР· РёСЃС…РѕРґРЅРёРєРѕРІ

=== FILE: proxy\balancer.py ===
import random
from collections import Counter

from typing import Dict, List, Iterator 


class _Balancer:
    def __init__(self):
        self.domains: List[str] = []
        self._dc_to_domain: Dict[int, str] = {}
    
    def update_domains_list(self, domains_list: List[str]) -> None:
        if Counter(self.domains) == Counter(domains_list):
            return
        
        self.domains = domains_list[:]

        self._dc_to_domain = {
            dc_id: random.choice(self.domains)
            for dc_id in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 203)
        }

    def update_domain_for_dc(self, dc_id: int, domain: str) -> bool:
        if self._dc_to_domain.get(dc_id) == domain:
            return False
        
        self._dc_to_domain[dc_id] = domain
        return True

    def get_domains_for_dc(self, dc_id: int) -> Iterator[str]:
        current_domain = self._dc_to_domain.get(dc_id)
        if current_domain is not None:
            yield current_domain

        shuffled_domains = self.domains[:]
        random.shuffle(shuffled_domains)

        for domain in shuffled_domains:
            if domain != current_domain:
                yield domain


balancer = _Balancer()

import asyncio
import logging
import struct
import time

from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Set, Tuple
from urllib.parse import urlencode

from .utils import *
from .stats import stats
from .balancer import balancer
from .config import proxy_config
from .raw_websocket import RawWebSocket
from .pool import cf_worker_pool
from .network_debug import WsActivity
from .cf_h2 import bridge_h2


log = logging.getLogger('tg-mtproto-proxy')
_st_I_le = struct.Struct('<I')

TCP_BACKOFF_INITIAL = 30.0
TCP_BACKOFF_MAX = 3600.0
_tcp_failures: Dict[Tuple[str, int], int] = {}
_tcp_retry_after: Dict[Tuple[str, int], float] = {}
_tcp_connecting: Set[Tuple[str, int]] = set()


def reset_tcp_backoff() -> None:
    _tcp_failures.clear()
    _tcp_retry_after.clear()
    _tcp_connecting.clear()


class CryptoCtx:
    __slots__ = ('clt_dec', 'clt_enc', 'tg_enc', 'tg_dec')

    def __init__(self, clt_dec, clt_enc, tg_enc, tg_dec):
        self.clt_dec = clt_dec  # decrypt from client
        self.clt_enc = clt_enc  # encrypt to client
        self.tg_enc = tg_enc    # encrypt to telegram
        self.tg_dec = tg_dec    # decrypt from telegram


class MsgSplitter:
    """
    Splits TCP stream data into individual MTProto transport packets
    so each can be sent as a separate WS frame.
    """
    __slots__ = ('_proto', '_cipher_buf', '_plain_buf', '_disabled')

    def __init__(self, proto_int: int):
        self._proto = proto_int
        self._cipher_buf = bytearray()
        self._plain_buf = bytearray()
        self._disabled = False

    def split(self, chunk: bytes, plain: bytes) -> List[bytes]:
        if not chunk:
            return []
        if self._disabled:
            return [chunk]

        self._cipher_buf.extend(chunk)
        self._plain_buf.extend(plain)

        parts = []
        offset = 0
        buf_len = len(self._cipher_buf)
        while offset < buf_len:
            packet_len = self._next_packet_len(offset, buf_len - offset)
            if packet_len is None:
                break
            if packet_len <= 0:
                parts.append(bytes(self._cipher_buf) if offset == 0 else bytes(self._cipher_buf[offset:]))
                offset = buf_len
                self._disabled = True
                break
            if offset == 0 and packet_len == buf_len:
                parts.append(bytes(self._cipher_buf))
            else:
                parts.append(bytes(self._cipher_buf[offset:offset + packet_len]))
            offset += packet_len

        if offset:
            del self._cipher_buf[:offset]
            del self._plain_buf[:offset]
        return parts

    def flush(self) -> List[bytes]:
        if not self._cipher_buf:
            return []
        tail = bytes(self._cipher_buf)
        self._cipher_buf.clear()
        self._plain_buf.clear()
        return [tail]

    def _next_packet_len(self, offset: int, avail: int) -> Optional[int]:
        if avail <= 0:
            return None
        if self._proto == PROTO_ABRIDGED_INT:
            return self._next_abridged_len(offset, avail)
        if self._proto in (PROTO_INTERMEDIATE_INT,
                           PROTO_PADDED_INTERMEDIATE_INT):
            return self._next_intermediate_len(offset, avail)
        return 0

    def _next_abridged_len(self, offset: int, avail: int) -> Optional[int]:
        first = self._plain_buf[offset]
        if first in (0x7F, 0xFF):
            if avail < 4:
                return None
            payload_len = int.from_bytes(
                self._plain_buf[offset + 1:offset + 4], 'little') * 4
            header_len = 4
        else:
            payload_len = (first & 0x7F) * 4
            header_len = 1
        if payload_len <= 0:
            return 0
        packet_len = header_len + payload_len
        if avail < packet_len:
            return None
        return packet_len

    def _next_intermediate_len(self, offset: int, avail: int) -> Optional[int]:
        if avail < 4:
            return None
        payload_len = _st_I_le.unpack_from(self._plain_buf, offset)[0] & 0x7FFFFFFF
        if payload_len <= 0:
            return 0
        packet_len = 4 + payload_len
        if avail < packet_len:
            return None
        return packet_len


async def do_fallback(reader, writer, relay_init, label,
                       dc: int, is_test_dc: bool, is_media: bool, media_tag: str,
                       ctx: CryptoCtx, splitter=None, *, h2_pool=None, proto_tag=None):
    ip_table = DC_TEST_IPS if is_test_dc else DC_DEFAULT_IPS
    fallback_dst = ip_table.get(dc)
    use_cf = proxy_config.fallback_cfproxy and not is_test_dc
    worker_domains = proxy_config.cfproxy_worker_domains

    methods: List[str] = []

    if worker_domains and fallback_dst:
        methods.append('cf_worker')
    if use_cf:
        methods.append('cf')
    if fallback_dst:
        methods.append('tcp')

    for method in methods:
        if method == 'cf_worker' and fallback_dst:
            ok = await _cfproxy_worker_fallback(
                reader, writer, relay_init, label, ctx,
                dc=dc, is_test_dc=is_test_dc, is_media=is_media,
                fallback_dst=fallback_dst, splitter=splitter)
            if ok:
                return True
        elif method == 'cf':
            if is_media and h2_pool is not None and proto_tag is not None:
                channel = await h2_pool.open(dc, label)
                if channel is not None:
                    stats.connections_h2 += 1
                    stats.connections_cfproxy += 1
                    await bridge_h2(reader, writer, channel, ctx, proto_tag)
                    return True
            ok = await _cfproxy_fallback(
                reader, writer, relay_init, label, ctx,
                dc=dc, is_media=is_media,
                splitter=splitter)
            if ok:
                return True
        elif method == 'tcp' and fallback_dst:
            ok = await _tcp_fallback(
                reader, writer, fallback_dst, 443,
                relay_init, label, ctx)
            if ok:
                return True
    return False


async def _cfproxy_worker_fallback(reader, writer, relay_init, label,
                                   ctx: CryptoCtx,
                                   dc: int, is_test_dc: bool, is_media: bool,
                                   fallback_dst: str,
                                   splitter=None):
    media_tag = ' media' if is_media else ''
    worker_domains = proxy_config.cfproxy_worker_domains
    if not worker_domains:
        return False

    pooled = None if is_test_dc else await cf_worker_pool.get(
        dc, fallback_dst, worker_domains)
    if pooled:
        ws, worker_domain = pooled
        log.info("[%s] DC%d%s -> CF worker pool hit via %s for %s",
                 label, dc, media_tag, worker_domain, fallback_dst)
    else:
        query = urlencode({
            'dst': fallback_dst,
            'dc': str(dc),
        })
        path = f'/apiws?{query}'

        ws = None
        for worker_domain in cf_worker_pool.available_domains(worker_domains):
            log.info("[%s] DC%d%s -> trying CF worker %s for %s",
                     label, dc, media_tag, worker_domain, fallback_dst)

            try:
                ws = await RawWebSocket.connect(worker_domain, worker_domain,
                                                timeout=10.0, path=path, 
                                                secure=not proxy_config.disable_secure)
                break
            except Exception as exc:
                cf_worker_pool.report_failure(worker_domain, exc)
                log.warning("[%s] DC%d%s CF worker %s failed: %s",
                            label, dc, media_tag, worker_domain, repr(exc))
                continue

        if ws is None:
            return False

    stats.connections_cfproxy += 1
    await ws.send(relay_init)
    await bridge_ws_reencrypt(reader, writer, ws, label, ctx,
                              dc=dc, is_media=is_media,
                              splitter=None)
    return True


async def _cfproxy_fallback(reader, writer, relay_init, label,
                            ctx: CryptoCtx,
                            dc: int, is_media: bool,
                            splitter=None):
    media_tag = ' media' if is_media else ''
    ws = None
    chosen_domain = None

    log.info("[%s] DC%d%s -> trying CF proxy",
            label, dc, media_tag)
    connect_started = time.monotonic()

    for base_domain in balancer.get_domains_for_dc(dc):
        domain = f'kws{dc}.{base_domain}'
        try:
            ws = await RawWebSocket.connect(domain, domain, timeout=10.0, 
                                            secure=not proxy_config.disable_secure)
            chosen_domain = base_domain
            break
        except Exception as exc:
            log.warning("[%s] DC%d%s CF proxy failed: %s",
                        label, dc, media_tag, repr(exc))

    if ws is None:
        return False

    log.debug('[%s] WS CONNECT DC%d%s route=cf setup_ms=%.0f',
              label, dc, media_tag, (time.monotonic() - connect_started) * 1000)
    if chosen_domain and balancer.update_domain_for_dc(dc, chosen_domain):
        log.info("[%s] Switched active CF domain", label)

    stats.connections_cfproxy += 1
    await ws.send(relay_init)
    await bridge_ws_reencrypt(reader, writer, ws, label, ctx,
                               dc=dc, is_media=is_media,
                               splitter=splitter)
    return True


async def _tcp_fallback(reader, writer, dst, port, relay_init, label, ctx: CryptoCtx):
    key = (dst, port)
    if key in _tcp_connecting or time.monotonic() < _tcp_retry_after.get(key, 0):
        log.debug("[%s] TCP fallback to %s:%d skipped (backoff or connecting)",
                  label, dst, port)
        return False

    _tcp_connecting.add(key)
    rw = None
    try:
        try:
            log.info("[%s] TCP fallback to %s:%d", label, dst, port)
            rr, rw = await asyncio.wait_for(
                asyncio.open_connection(dst, port), timeout=10)
            rw.write(relay_init)
            await rw.drain()
        except Exception as exc:
            failures = _tcp_failures.get(key, 0) + 1
            _tcp_failures[key] = failures
            delay = min(TCP_BACKOFF_INITIAL * 2 ** min(failures - 1, 7),
                        TCP_BACKOFF_MAX)
            _tcp_retry_after[key] = time.monotonic() + delay
            log.warning("[%s] TCP fallback to %s:%d failed: %r; retry in %.0fs",
                        label, dst, port, exc, delay)
            return False
        finally:
            _tcp_connecting.discard(key)

        _tcp_failures.pop(key, None)
        _tcp_retry_after.pop(key, None)
        stats.connections_tcp_fallback += 1
        await _bridge_tcp_reencrypt(reader, writer, rr, rw, label, ctx)
        return True
    finally:
        if rw is not None:
            rw.close()
            try:
                await rw.wait_closed()
            except (OSError, ConnectionError):
                pass


async def bridge_ws_reencrypt(reader, writer, ws: RawWebSocket, label,
                               ctx: CryptoCtx,
                               dc=None, is_media=False,
                               splitter: Optional[MsgSplitter] = None):
    """
    Bidirectional TCP(client) <-> WS(telegram) with re-encryption.
    client ciphertext в†’ decrypt(clt_key) в†’ encrypt(tg_key) в†’ WS
    WS data в†’ decrypt(tg_key) в†’ encrypt(clt_key) в†’ client TCP
    """
    dc_tag = f"DC{dc}{'m' if is_media else ''}" if dc else "DC?"

    up_bytes = 0
    down_bytes = 0
    up_packets = 0
    down_packets = 0
    start_time = asyncio.get_running_loop().time()
    close_reason = 'normal'
    activity = WsActivity(label, dc_tag) if log.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG) else None

    async def tcp_to_ws():
        nonlocal up_bytes, up_packets, close_reason
        try:
            while True:
                chunk = await reader.read(65536)
                if not chunk:
                    if splitter:
                        tail = splitter.flush()
                        if tail:
                            if activity is not None:
                                activity.send_started()
                            await ws.send(tail[0])
                            if activity is not None:
                                activity.sent(len(tail[0]))
                    break
                n = len(chunk)
                if activity is not None:
                    activity.input(n)
                stats.bytes_up += n
                up_bytes += n
                up_packets += 1
                plain = ctx.clt_dec.update(chunk)
                chunk = ctx.tg_enc.update(plain)
                if splitter:
                    parts = splitter.split(chunk, plain)
                    if not parts:
                        continue
                    if activity is not None:
                        activity.send_started()
                    if len(parts) > 1:
                        await ws.send_batch(parts)
                    else:
                        await ws.send(parts[0])
                    if activity is not None:
                        activity.sent(sum(map(len, parts)))
                else:
                    if activity is not None:
                        activity.send_started()
                    await ws.send(chunk)
                    if activity is not None:
                        activity.sent(len(chunk))
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            return
        except (ConnectionError, OSError) as e:
            close_reason = f"client: {type(e).__name__}"
        except Exception as e:
            close_reason = f"client: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"
            log.debug("[%s] tcp->ws ended: %s", label, e)

    async def ws_to_tcp():
        nonlocal down_bytes, down_packets, close_reason
        try:
            while True:
                data = await ws.recv()
                if data is None:
                    if close_reason == 'normal':
                        close_reason = 'upstream: ws_close'
                    break
                n = len(data)
                if activity is not None:
                    activity.received(n)
                stats.bytes_down += n
                down_bytes += n
                down_packets += 1
                plain = ctx.tg_dec.update(data)
                data = ctx.clt_enc.update(plain)
                writer.write(data)
                await writer.drain()
                if activity is not None:
                    activity.delivered(n)
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            return
        except (ConnectionError, OSError) as e:
            close_reason = f"upstream: {type(e).__name__}"
        except asyncio.IncompleteReadError:
            close_reason = 'upstream: tcp_reset'
        except Exception as e:
            close_reason = f"upstream: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"
            log.debug("[%s] ws->tcp ended: %s", label, e)

    tasks = [asyncio.create_task(tcp_to_ws()),
             asyncio.create_task(ws_to_tcp())]
    try:
        await asyncio.wait(tasks, return_when=asyncio.FIRST_COMPLETED)
    finally:
        for t in tasks:
            t.cancel()
        for t in tasks:
            try:
                await t
            except BaseException:
                pass
        elapsed = asyncio.get_running_loop().time() - start_time
        if activity is not None:
            activity.close()
        log.info("[%s] %s WS session closed (%s): "
                 "^%s (%d pkts) v%s (%d pkts) in %.1fs",
                 label, dc_tag, close_reason,
                 human_bytes(up_bytes), up_packets,
                 human_bytes(down_bytes), down_packets,
                 elapsed)
        try:
            await ws.close()
        except BaseException:
            pass
        try:
            writer.close()
            await writer.wait_closed()
        except BaseException:
            pass


async def _bridge_tcp_reencrypt(reader, writer, remote_reader, remote_writer,
                                label, ctx: CryptoCtx):
    """Bidirectional TCP <-> TCP with re-encryption."""

    async def forward(src, dst_w, is_up):
        try:
            while True:
                data = await src.read(65536)
                if not data:
                    break
                n = len(data)
                if is_up:
                    stats.bytes_up += n
                    plain = ctx.clt_dec.update(data)
                    data = ctx.tg_enc.update(plain)
                else:
                    stats.bytes_down += n
                    plain = ctx.tg_dec.update(data)
                    data = ctx.clt_enc.update(plain)
                dst_w.write(data)
                await dst_w.drain()
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            pass
        except Exception as e:
            log.debug("[%s] forward ended: %s", label, e)

    tasks = [
        asyncio.create_task(forward(reader, remote_writer, True)),
        asyncio.create_task(forward(remote_reader, writer, False)),
    ]
    try:
        await asyncio.wait(tasks, return_when=asyncio.FIRST_COMPLETED)
    finally:
        for t in tasks:
            t.cancel()
        for t in tasks:
            try:
                await t
            except BaseException:
                pass
        for w in (writer, remote_writer):
            try:
                w.close()
                await w.wait_closed()
            except BaseException:
                pass

from __future__ import annotations

    DC_DEFAULT_IPS, PROTO_TAG_ABRIDGED,
    PROTO_TAG_SECURE,
)

log = logging.getLogger('tg-mtproto-proxy')
MAX_PACKET = 4 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_PLAIN_PADDING = 256
MAX_CHANNEL_REQUESTS = 8
MAX_CHANNEL_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_LANE_REQUESTS = 64
MAX_LANE_BYTES = 32 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_LANE_REPLY_BYTES = 32 * 1024 * 1024
DOMAIN_COOLDOWN = 30.0
REQUEST_TIMEOUT = 65.0
